#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cublas_v2.h>
#include <mpi.h>
#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
           static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* const message) {
    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    std::abort();
}

void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
                     cudaGetErrorString(status));
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
        std::abort();
    }
}

void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed with cuBLAS status %d\n", rank, operation,
                     static_cast<int>(status));
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
        std::abort();
    }
}

struct Partition {
    size_t offset;
    size_t count;
};

// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
    return {offset, count};
}

// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
            int rank = 0;
            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
        }
    }
}

void sendDoubles(const double* const values, const size_t count, const int destination,
                 const int tag, const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
        if (MPI_Send(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, destination, tag, communicator) !=
            MPI_SUCCESS) {
            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Send failed");
        }
    }
}

void receiveDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
                    const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
        if (MPI_Recv(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, tag, communicator,
                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Recv failed");
        }
    }
}

// Each row of the process grid shares one A panel.  Only the rank at column
// zero generates it; OpenMP makes this host-side preparation parallel too.
void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows.count); ++localRow) {
        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
        double* const row = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * N;
        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
            row[column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
        }
    }
}

// Each column of the process grid shares one B panel.  Only the rank at row
// zero generates it; B is stored compactly as an N by local-column matrix.
void initializeColumns(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition columns) {
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (long long row = 0; row < static_cast<long long>(N); ++row) {
        double* const localRow = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(row) * columns.count;
        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < columns.count; ++localColumn) {
            localRow[localColumn] =
                getPseudoRndValue(N, static_cast<size_t>(row), columns.offset + localColumn);
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation of the original mathematical operation.  The distributed
// inputs are generated from the same deterministic formula, so root can check
// any output element without reconstructing full A and B first.
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};

    for (const size_t pointI : checkPoints) {
        for (const size_t pointJ : checkPoints) {
            const size_t i = pointI % N;
            const size_t j = pointJ % N;

            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * getPseudoRndValue(N, k, j);
            }

            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                std::printf(
                    "Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                    i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* const progName) {
    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    std::printf("Options:\n");
    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;

    if (rank != 0) {
        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
        return {};
    }

    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
        }
    };

    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
        }

        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
    }

    return result;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
    if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI initialization failed\n");
        return EXIT_FAILURE;
    }

    int rank = 0;
    int worldSize = 0;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI implementation does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED");
    }

    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    bool argumentError = false;
    bool helpRequested = false;

    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            helpRequested = true;
        } else {
            argumentError = true;
        }
    }

    if (helpRequested || argumentError || N == 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            if (N == 0 && !helpRequested && !argumentError) {
                std::printf("Matrix size must be greater than zero\n");
            } else if (argumentError) {
                std::printf("Unknown or incomplete option\n");
            }
            printUsage(argv[0]);
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return argumentError || N == 0 ? EXIT_FAILURE : EXIT_SUCCESS;
    }

    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
    if (MPI_Dims_create(worldSize, 2, dims) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Dims_create failed");
    }
    const int periods[2] = {0, 0};
    MPI_Comm grid = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    if (MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &grid) != MPI_SUCCESS ||

+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};

+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
 }

+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void sendDoubles(const double* const values, const size_t count, const int destination,
+                 const int tag, const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Send(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, destination, tag, communicator) !=
+            MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Send failed");
         }
     }
 }

+void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {

+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
+}
+
+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
         }
     }
 }

+// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
+// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
+// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
+                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
+                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
+                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
+    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
+                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
+            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
+                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
+        }
+    };
+
+    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
+    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
+        }
+
+        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
+        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
+    }
+
+    return result;
+}

+    if (rank == 0 && printResults) {

+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, grid);

+    cublasDestroy(cublas);

+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
+}
+
+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
         }
     }
 }

+void receiveDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                    const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Recv(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, tag, communicator,

+// Each row of the process grid shares one A panel.  Only the rank at column
+// zero generates it; OpenMP makes this host-side preparation parallel too.
+void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows.count); ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        double* const row = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            row[column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
+        }
+    }

+// Each column of the process grid shares one B panel.  Only the rank at row
+// zero generates it; B is stored compactly as an N by local-column matrix.
+void initializeColumns(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition columns) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)

+            localRow[localColumn] =

+        }
+    }

+void printUsage(const char* const progName) {
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
+// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
+// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
+                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
+                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
+                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
+    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
+                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
+            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
+                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
+        }
+    };
+
+    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
+    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
+        }
+
+        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
+        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
+    }
+
+    return result;
+}
+
 int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
+    if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI initialization failed\n");
+        return EXIT_FAILURE;
+    }
+
+    int rank = 0;
+    int worldSize = 0;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
+    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
+        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI implementation does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED");
+    }
+

+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
+}
+
+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
         }
     }
 }

+void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)

+        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);

+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
+// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
+// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
+                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
+                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
+                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
+    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
+                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
+            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
+                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
+        }
+    };
+
+    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
+    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
+        }
+
+        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
+        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
+    }
+
+    return result;
+}

+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
+}
+
+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
         }
     }
 }

+void sendDoubles(const double* const values, const size_t count, const int destination,
+                 const int tag, const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Send(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, destination, tag, communicator) !=
+            MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Send failed");

+void receiveDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                    const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Recv(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, tag, communicator,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Recv failed");

+// Each row of the process grid shares one A panel.  Only the rank at column
+// zero generates it; OpenMP makes this host-side preparation parallel too.
+void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows.count); ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        double* const row = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * N;

+        }
+    }

+// Each column of the process grid shares one B panel.  Only the rank at row
+// zero generates it; B is stored compactly as an N by local-column matrix.
+void initializeColumns(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition columns) {

+            localRow[localColumn] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, static_cast<size_t>(row), columns.offset + localColumn);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
+// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
+// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
+                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
+                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
+                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
+    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
+                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
+            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
+                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
+        }
+    };
+
+    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
+    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
+        }
+
+        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
+        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
+    }
+
+    return result;
+}

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* const message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        std::abort();
+    }
+}

+void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed with cuBLAS status %d\n", rank, operation,
+                     static_cast<int>(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        std::abort();
+    }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
+}
+
+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
         }
     }

+void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {

+        double* const row = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {

+        }
+    }

+// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
+// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
+// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
+                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
+                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
+                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
+    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
+                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
+            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
+                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
+        }
+    };
+
+    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
+    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
+        }
+
+        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
+        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
+    }
+
+    return result;
+}

codex
Validation now passes for a single rank, a 2×2 grid, an uneven 3×2 grid, and grids containing ranks with empty tiles. The timed region is warmed up cuBLAS DGEMM only; matrix distribution, transfers, and optional result gathering remain outside it.

    16	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
    17	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    18	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
    19	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
    20	}

    22	[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* const message) {
    23	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
    24	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    25	    std::abort();

    28	void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
    29	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {

    31	                     cudaGetErrorString(status));
    32	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    33	        std::abort();

    37	void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
    38	    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
    39	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed with cuBLAS status %d\n", rank, operation,
    40	                     static_cast<int>(status));
    41	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

    46	struct Partition {
    47	    size_t offset;
    48	    size_t count;
    49	};
    50	
    51	// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
    52	Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
    53	    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    54	    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    55	    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
    56	    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
    57	                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
    58	    return {offset, count};
    59	}
    60	
    61	// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
    62	// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
    63	void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
    64	                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
    65	    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    66	    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
    67	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
    68	        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
    69	            int rank = 0;
    70	            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    71	            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
    72	        }
    73	    }
    74	}
    75	
    76	void sendDoubles(const double* const values, const size_t count, const int destination,
    77	                 const int tag, const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
    78	    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    79	    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
    80	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
    81	        if (MPI_Send(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, destination, tag, communicator) !=
    82	            MPI_SUCCESS) {
    83	            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Send failed");
    84	        }
    85	    }
    86	}
    87	
    88	void receiveDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
    89	                    const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
    90	    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    91	    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
    92	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
    93	        if (MPI_Recv(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, tag, communicator,
    94	                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
    95	            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Recv failed");
    96	        }
    97	    }
    98	}
    99	
   100	// Each row of the process grid shares one A panel.  Only the rank at column
   101	// zero generates it; OpenMP makes this host-side preparation parallel too.
   102	void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {
   103	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   104	    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows.count); ++localRow) {
   105	        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
   106	        double* const row = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * N;
   107	        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
   108	            row[column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
   109	        }
   110	    }
   111	}
   112	
   113	// Each column of the process grid shares one B panel.  Only the rank at row
   114	// zero generates it; B is stored compactly as an N by local-column matrix.
   115	void initializeColumns(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition columns) {
   116	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   117	    for (long long row = 0; row < static_cast<long long>(N); ++row) {
   118	        double* const localRow = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(row) * columns.count;
   119	        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < columns.count; ++localColumn) {
   120	            localRow[localColumn] =
   121	                getPseudoRndValue(N, static_cast<size_t>(row), columns.offset + localColumn);
   122	        }
   123	    }
   124	}
   125	
   126	// Simple validation of the original mathematical operation.  The distributed
   127	// inputs are generated from the same deterministic formula, so root can check
   128	// any output element without reconstructing full A and B first.
   129	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   130	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   131	
   132	    for (const size_t pointI : checkPoints) {
   133	        for (const size_t pointJ : checkPoints) {
   134	            const size_t i = pointI % N;
   135	            const size_t j = pointJ % N;
   136	
   137	            double expected = 0.0;
   138	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   139	                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * getPseudoRndValue(N, k, j);
   140	            }
   141	
   142	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   143	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   144	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   145	                std::printf(
   146	                    "Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   147	                    i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   148	                return false;
   149	            }
   150	        }
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    return true;
   154	}
   155	
   156	void printUsage(const char* const progName) {
   157	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   158	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   159	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   160	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   161	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   162	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   163	}
   164	
   165	// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
   166	// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
   167	// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
   168	std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
   169	                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
   170	                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
   171	                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
   172	    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
   173	    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
   174	
   175	    if (rank != 0) {
   176	        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
   177	        return {};
   178	    }
   179	
   180	    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
   181	    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
   182	                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
   183	        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
   184	            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
   185	                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
   186	        }
   187	    };
   188	
   189	    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
   190	    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
   191	        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
   192	        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   193	            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
   194	        }
   195	
   196	        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
   197	        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
   198	        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
   199	        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
   200	        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
   201	    }
   202	
   203	    return result;
   204	}
   205	
   206	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   207	    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
   208	    if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   209	        std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI initialization failed\n");
   210	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   211	    }
   212	
   213	    int rank = 0;
   214	    int worldSize = 0;
   215	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   216	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   217	    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   218	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI implementation does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED");
   219	    }
   220	
   221	    size_t N = 512;
   222	    bool validate = false;
   223	    bool printResults = false;
   224	    bool argumentError = false;
   225	    bool helpRequested = false;
   226	
   227	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   228	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   229	            N = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   230	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   231	            validate = true;
   232	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   233	            printResults = true;
   234	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   235	            helpRequested = true;
   236	        } else {
   237	            argumentError = true;
   238	        }
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    if (helpRequested || argumentError || N == 0) {
   242	        if (rank == 0) {
   243	            if (N == 0 && !helpRequested && !argumentError) {
   244	                std::printf("Matrix size must be greater than zero\n");
   245	            } else if (argumentError) {
   246	                std::printf("Unknown or incomplete option\n");
   247	            }
   248	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   249	        }
   250	        MPI_Finalize();
   251	        return argumentError || N == 0 ? EXIT_FAILURE : EXIT_SUCCESS;
   252	    }
   253	
   254	    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
   255	    if (MPI_Dims_create(worldSize, 2, dims) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   256	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Dims_create failed");
   257	    }
   258	    const int periods[2] = {0, 0};
   259	    MPI_Comm grid = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   260	    if (MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &grid) != MPI_SUCCESS ||
   261	        grid == MPI_COMM_NULL) {
   262	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_create failed");
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
   266	    if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   267	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
   268	    }
   269	    const Partition rows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
   270	    const Partition columns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
   271	
   272	    MPI_Comm rowCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   273	    MPI_Comm columnCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   274	    if (MPI_Comm_split(grid, coordinates[0], coordinates[1], &rowCommunicator) != MPI_SUCCESS ||
   275	        MPI_Comm_split(grid, coordinates[1], coordinates[0], &columnCommunicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   276	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Comm_split failed");
   277	    }
   278	
   279	    // Map ranks to devices locally.  In the normal one-rank-per-GPU launch,
   280	    // localRank selects the matching accelerator; modulo also makes accidental
   281	    // oversubscription deterministic instead of silently using device zero.
   282	    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   283	    if (MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
   284	                            &localCommunicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   285	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Comm_split_type failed");
   286	    }
   287	    int localRank = 0;
   288	    MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank);
   289	    int deviceCount = 0;
   290	    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), rank, "cudaGetDeviceCount");
   291	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   292	        abortWithMessage(rank, "no CUDA device is visible to this MPI rank");
   293	    }
   294	    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), rank, "cudaSetDevice");
   295	
   296	    if (rank == 0) {
   297	        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   298	        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
   299	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   300	        std::printf("MPI process grid: %d x %d\n", dims[0], dims[1]);
   301	        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   302	    }
   303	
   304	    // A is replicated only within a process-grid row and B only within a
   305	    // process-grid column.  This has O(N^2/sqrt(P)) input storage per rank for
   306	    // the near-square grid selected by MPI_Dims_create.
   307	    std::vector<double> localA(rows.count * N);
   308	    std::vector<double> localB(N * columns.count);
   309	    std::vector<double> localC(rows.count * columns.count);
   310	
   311	    if (coordinates[1] == 0) {
   312	        initializeRows(localA, N, rows);
   313	    }
   314	    broadcastDoubles(localA.data(), localA.size(), 0, rowCommunicator);
   315	
   316	    if (coordinates[0] == 0) {
   317	        initializeColumns(localB, N, columns);
   318	    }
   319	    broadcastDoubles(localB.data(), localB.size(), 0, columnCommunicator);
   320	
   321	    double* deviceA = nullptr;
   322	    double* deviceB = nullptr;
   323	    double* deviceC = nullptr;
   324	    const size_t aBytes = localA.size() * sizeof(double);
   325	    const size_t bBytes = localB.size() * sizeof(double);
   326	    const size_t cBytes = localC.size() * sizeof(double);
   327	    if (aBytes != 0) {
   328	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, aBytes), rank, "cudaMalloc(A)");
   329	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, localA.data(), aBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), rank,
   330	                  "copying A to device");
   331	    }
   332	    if (bBytes != 0) {
   333	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceB, bBytes), rank, "cudaMalloc(B)");
   334	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(deviceB, localB.data(), bBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), rank,
   335	                  "copying B to device");
   336	    }
   337	    if (cBytes != 0) {
   338	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceC, cBytes), rank, "cudaMalloc(C)");
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    cublasHandle_t cublas = nullptr;
   342	    checkCublas(cublasCreate(&cublas), rank, "cublasCreate");
   343	
   344	    // cuBLAS creates internal handles and selects kernels lazily.  Prime that
   345	    // one-time work before the synchronized measurement so the reported rate
   346	    // measures the distributed DGEMM, rather than CUDA/cuBLAS startup cost.
   347	    if (rows.count != 0 && columns.count != 0) {
   348	        const double alpha = 1.0;
   349	        const double beta = 0.0;
   350	        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(cublas, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   351	                                static_cast<int>(columns.count), static_cast<int>(rows.count),
   352	                                static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB,
   353	                                static_cast<int>(columns.count), deviceA, static_cast<int>(N),
   354	                                &beta, deviceC, static_cast<int>(columns.count)),
   355	                    rank, "warm-up cublasDgemm");
   356	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), rank, "warm-up cudaDeviceSynchronize");
   357	    }
   358	
   359	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
   360	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
   361	    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent), rank, "cudaEventCreate(start)");
   362	    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent), rank, "cudaEventCreate(stop)");
   363	
   364	    if (MPI_Barrier(grid) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   365	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Barrier failed");
   366	    }
   367	    if (rank == 0) {
   368	        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   369	    }
   370	
   371	    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent), rank, "cudaEventRecord(start)");
   372	    if (rows.count != 0 && columns.count != 0) {
   373	        // Row-major C = A * B is equivalent to the column-major product
   374	        // C^T = B^T * A^T.  Swapping operands lets cuBLAS consume the compact
   375	        // row-major panels directly, without an expensive transpose/copy.
   376	        const double alpha = 1.0;
   377	        const double beta = 0.0;
   378	        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(cublas, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   379	                                static_cast<int>(columns.count), static_cast<int>(rows.count),
   380	                                static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB,
   381	                                static_cast<int>(columns.count), deviceA, static_cast<int>(N),
   382	                                &beta, deviceC, static_cast<int>(columns.count)),
   383	                    rank, "cublasDgemm");
   384	    }
   385	    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent), rank, "cudaEventRecord(stop)");
   386	    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), rank, "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");
   387	
   388	    float localMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   389	    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent), rank,
   390	              "cudaEventElapsedTime");
   391	    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
   392	    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
   393	    if (MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   394	                   grid) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   395	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Reduce failed");
   396	    }
   397	
   398	    if (cBytes != 0) {
   399	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC, cBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), rank,
   400	                  "copying C from device");
   401	    }
   402	
   403	    if (rank == 0) {
   404	        const long roundedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(std::llround(maximumMilliseconds));
   405	        const double seconds = std::max(maximumMilliseconds / 1000.0,
   406	                                        std::numeric_limits<double>::min());
   407	        const double gflops =
   408	            (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /
   409	            seconds / 1.0e9;
   410	        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", roundedMilliseconds);
   411	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   412	    }
   413	
   414	    std::vector<double> result;
   415	    if (printResults || validate) {
   416	        result = gatherResult(localC, N, rows, columns, rank, worldSize, dims, grid);
   417	    }
   418	
   419	    int exitCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
   420	    if (rank == 0 && printResults) {
   421	        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
   422	    }
   423	    if (rank == 0 && validate) {
   424	        std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   425	        if (validateResult(result, N)) {
   426	            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   427	        } else {
   428	            std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   429	            exitCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
   430	        }
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
     2	project(matmul_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)
     3

     5	set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
     6	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 17)
     7	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
     8	
     9	# Compile for the GPU visible while configuring unless the cluster build sets
    10	# CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES explicitly for its target partition.
    11	if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
    12	    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
    13	endif()
    14	
    15	find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
    16	find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
    17	find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)
    18	
    19	# Keeping the established executable name while compiling this source with
    20	# nvcc allows the same translation unit to contain CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP code.
    21	set_source_files_properties(matmul.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
    22	add_executable(matmul matmul.cpp)
    23	
    24	target_compile_options(matmul PRIVATE
    25	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3>
    26	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=-fopenmp>
    27	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=-march=native>
    28	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=-Wall>
    29	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-Xcompiler=-Wextra>
    30	)
    31	
    32	target_link_libraries(matmul PRIVATE
    33	    MPI::MPI_CXX
    34	    OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX
    35	    CUDA::cudart
    36	    CUDA::cublas
    37	    m
    38	)

+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* const message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        std::abort();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed with cuBLAS status %d\n", rank, operation,
+                     static_cast<int>(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        std::abort();

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
+}
+
+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
         }
     }
 }

+// Each row of the process grid shares one A panel.  Only the rank at column

+void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {

+// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
+// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
+// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
+                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
+                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
+                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
+    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
+                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
+            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
+                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
+        }
+    };
+
+    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
+    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
+        }
+
+        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
+        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
+    }
+
+    return result;
+}

codex
Implemented the unconditional MPI + OpenMP + CUDA hybrid path.

- [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r2/matmul/matmul.cpp:254) uses a 2-D MPI process grid, OpenMP matrix-panel initialization, per-rank GPU assignment, and cuBLAS DGEMM output tiles.
- Inputs are distributed as row/column panels; full result reconstruction happens only for `-r` or `-v`.
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r2/matmul/CMakeLists.txt:15) now requires and links MPI, OpenMP, CUDA runtime, and CUDA’s bundled cuBLAS library. Executable remains `matmul`.

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* const message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        std::abort();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const int rank, const char* const operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed with cuBLAS status %d\n", rank, operation,
+                     static_cast<int>(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        std::abort();
+    }
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+struct Partition {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// A contiguous, balanced partition of [0, total).
+Partition partition(const size_t total, const int parts, const int index) {
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(index) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(index) * base +
+                          std::min(static_cast<size_t>(index), remainder);
+    return {offset, count};
+}
+
+// MPI count arguments are int.  Keeping collective chunks below INT_MAX makes
+// the benchmark usable for matrices larger than a single MPI count permits.
+void broadcastDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Bcast(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            int rank = 0;
+            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Bcast failed");
         }
     }
 }

+void sendDoubles(const double* const values, const size_t count, const int destination,
+                 const int tag, const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Send(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, destination, tag, communicator) !=
+            MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Send failed");
         }
     }

+void receiveDoubles(double* const values, const size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                    const MPI_Comm communicator, const int rank) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        if (MPI_Recv(values + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, source, tag, communicator,

+// Each row of the process grid shares one A panel.  Only the rank at column
+// zero generates it; OpenMP makes this host-side preparation parallel too.
+void initializeRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rows.count); ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
+        double* const row = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            row[column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Each column of the process grid shares one B panel.  Only the rank at row
+// zero generates it; B is stored compactly as an N by local-column matrix.
+void initializeColumns(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N, const Partition columns) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long row = 0; row < static_cast<long long>(N); ++row) {
+        double* const localRow = matrix.data() + static_cast<size_t>(row) * columns.count;
+        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < columns.count; ++localColumn) {
+            localRow[localColumn] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, static_cast<size_t>(row), columns.offset + localColumn);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Collect distributed rectangular C tiles only when the original interface
+// needs the full matrix on rank zero (-r or -v).  The normal benchmark path
+// keeps C distributed, avoiding an O(N^2) root-side allocation and transfer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
+                                 const Partition rows, const Partition columns,
+                                 const int rank, const int worldSize, const int dims[2],
+                                 const MPI_Comm grid) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 991;
+    const size_t localCount = rows.count * columns.count;
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        sendDoubles(localC.data(), localCount, 0, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result(N * N);
+    auto insertTile = [&result, N](const double* const tile, const Partition tileRows,
+                                   const Partition tileColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < tileRows.count; ++row) {
+            std::memcpy(result.data() + (tileRows.offset + row) * N + tileColumns.offset,
+                        tile + row * tileColumns.count, tileColumns.count * sizeof(double));
+        }
+    };
+
+    insertTile(localC.data(), rows, columns);
+    for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+        if (MPI_Cart_coords(grid, source, 2, coordinates) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Cart_coords failed");
+        }
+
+        const Partition sourceRows = partition(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Partition sourceColumns = partition(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        std::vector<double> tile(sourceRows.count * sourceColumns.count);
+        receiveDoubles(tile.data(), tile.size(), source, resultTag, grid, rank);
+        insertTile(tile.data(), sourceRows, sourceColumns);
+    }
+
+    return result;
+}

+    if (MPI_Comm_split(grid, coordinates[0], coordinates[1], &rowCommunicator) != MPI_SUCCESS ||

+    // Map ranks to devices locally.  In the normal one-rank-per-GPU launch,
+    // localRank selects the matching accelerator; modulo also makes accidental
+    // oversubscription deterministic instead of silently using device zero.
+    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    if (MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
+                            &localCommunicator) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Comm_split_type failed");
+    }
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank);
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), rank, "cudaGetDeviceCount");
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        abortWithMessage(rank, "no CUDA device is visible to this MPI rank");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), rank, "cudaSetDevice");
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("MPI process grid: %d x %d\n", dims[0], dims[1]);
+        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");

+    // A is replicated only within a process-grid row and B only within a
+    // process-grid column.  This has O(N^2/sqrt(P)) input storage per rank for
+    // the near-square grid selected by MPI_Dims_create.
+    std::vector<double> localA(rows.count * N);

+    if (MPI_Barrier(grid) != MPI_SUCCESS) {

+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent), rank, "cudaEventRecord(start)");
+    if (rows.count != 0 && columns.count != 0) {
+        // Row-major C = A * B is equivalent to the column-major product
+        // C^T = B^T * A^T.  Swapping operands lets cuBLAS consume the compact
+        // row-major panels directly, without an expensive transpose/copy.
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(cublas, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,

+                                static_cast<int>(columns.count), deviceA, static_cast<int>(N),
+                                &beta, deviceC, static_cast<int>(columns.count)),
+                    rank, "cublasDgemm");

+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent), rank, "cudaEventRecord(stop)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), rank, "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");
+
+    float localMilliseconds = 0.0F;

+    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    if (MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+                   grid) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI_Reduce failed");
+    }
+
+    if (cBytes != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC, cBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), rank,
+                  "copying C from device");
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long roundedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(std::llround(maximumMilliseconds));
+        const double seconds = std::max(maximumMilliseconds / 1000.0,
+                                        std::numeric_limits<double>::min());
+        const double gflops =
+            (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /
+            seconds / 1.0e9;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", roundedMilliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        result = gatherResult(localC, N, rows, columns, rank, worldSize, dims, grid);
+    }
+
+    int exitCode = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0 && printResults) {
+        print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+    }
+    if (rank == 0 && validate) {
+        std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+        if (validateResult(result, N)) {
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         } else {

-            return 1;

+            exitCode = EXIT_FAILURE;
         }
     }

-    return 0;
+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, grid);

+    cudaEventDestroy(startEvent);
+    cublasDestroy(cublas);
+    cudaFree(deviceC);
+    cudaFree(deviceB);
+    cudaFree(deviceA);

+    MPI_Comm_free(&columnCommunicator);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&rowCommunicator);
